Business Model and Strategic Direction
業務模式及策略方向

The Group is a leading Information, Communication and Technology (“ICT”)          本集團為一間領先的資訊、通訊及科技（
                                                                                                   「資訊
products and services provider, serving a wide regional customer base. The        通訊科技」）產品及服務供應商，為龐大的地區
Group has over 80 offices in nine countries namely China, Thailand, Malaysia,     客 戶 群 服 務。本 集 團 逾80個 辦 事 處 遍 及 中 國、

LABOUR STANDARDS                                                                      勞工準則
The Group strongly condemns any form of child and forced labour, as it                本集團強烈譴責任何形式的童工及強制勞工，
violates international labour standards. The Group adheres strictly to the            因為其違反國際勞工公約。本集團嚴格遵守相
relevant laws and regulations in the jurisdictions where it operates, concerning      關司法權區有關童工及強制勞工之相關規則及
child and forced labour. The Group prohibits the use of child and forced              規例。本集團禁止在所有經營業務中使用童工
labour in all its operations. The Group provides equal opportunities and fair         及強制勞工。本集團為所有僱員與應徵者提供
treatment to all employees and job applicants.                                        平等機會及公平待遇。


To ensure compliance, the Human Resource Department conducts                          為確保遵守規定，人力資源部對所有應徵者進
background checks on all job applicants. Each potential job applicant is              行背景調查。每位有意應徵者均須填寫職位申
required to complete an employment application form, which the Human                  請表，人力資源部會審閱申請表以證實所提供
Resource Department reviews to verify the accuracy of the information                 資料之準確性。人力資源部負責監管僱傭並防
provided. The Human Resource Department is responsible for regulating                 止出現任何不合規行為。如出現人力資源部無
employment and preventing any non-compliant behaviours. If any cases                  法處理之任何情況，應立即向上級管理層報告
arise that cannot be handled by the Human Resource Department, they are               以採取進一步行動。
promptly reported to upper management for further action.


We form a voluntary employment relationship with each of our employees and            我們與每名僱員建立自願僱傭關係，工作時間
working hours exceeding the regulatory limits is not allowed. We also promote         不得超過法定限額。我們亦於供應鏈內部推廣
such concepts within the supply chain so as to protect the wellbeing of labour        這 一 理 念，保 障 整 個 組 織 內 的 勞 工 福 祉。我 們
of the entire community. We hope to spread out the ideas of restricting child         希望宣揚限制童工及強制勞工的理念。如不幸
and force labour. If, unfortunately child labour is found, their employment will      發現童工，將立即終止僱傭並根據已商定工資
be terminated immediately and remuneration will be made according to the              支付薪酬。
agreed wages.


During the Reporting Period, the Group did not recognize any material                 於報告期內，本集團並無發現任何違反法律法
irregularities in the laws and regulations relating to any labour standards.          規中任何勞工準則的重大違規行為。

other stakeholders. Accordingly, the Group has established an labour safety           相關法律及法規，本集團已建立勞工安全與健

more to the society and help the less privileged groups in society. The Group       社 會，幫 助 社 會 弱 勢 群 體。本 集 團 將 社 會 福 利
prioritizes social welfare, particularly in supporting vulnerable groups like       放 在 首 位，特 別 是 在 支 持 長 者、兒 童 及 病 患 等

CLIMATE CHANGE                                                                    氣候變化
Climate change is an issue that is plaguing many businesses and countries         氣候變化是困擾全球眾多企業及國家的問題。
worldwide. The Group is not likely to be unaffected and will not leave            本 集 團 不 可 能 不 受 影 響，亦 不 會 無 所 防 備。除
ourselves unguarded. In addition to reducing GHG emissions, we have also          減少溫室氣體排放外，我們還制定了適應措施。
implemented adaptation measures.


According to the Taskforce on Climate-related Financial Disclosures (“TCFD”)      根據氣候相關財務信息披露工作組的框架，氣
framework, climate-related risks can be categorised as physical risks and         候相關風險可分為物理風險及過渡風險。在氣
transition risks. Physical risks can be acute or chronic in climate patterns.     候 模 式 中，物 理 風 險 可 能 是 急 性 的，也 可 能 是
Transition risks can be evolved from transition to a lower-carbon economy.        慢性的。過渡風險可以從向低碳經濟的過渡演
                                                                                  變而來。


Due to our Group’s business nature is mainly distribution of IT products, the     由於本集團的業務性質主要是分銷資訊科技產
supply chain may be disrupted by extreme weather events, such as typhoon.         品，供應鏈可能因颱風等極端天氣情況而中斷。
Any decrease in capacity of timely delivery may result in a loss of revenue.      及時交付的能力出現任何下降都可能導致損失
                                                                                  收入。

Permitted Indemnity Provisions                                                         獲准許彌償條文

of the assets of the Company against all losses and liabilities which they may         任何已作出、同意或遺漏之行為而招致或蒙受
incur or sustain by or by reason of any act done, concurred in or omitted in or        之所有損失及責任，從本公司資產中獲得彌償
about the execution of their duty. The Company has arranged for appropriate            及 受 到 保 障。本 公 司 已 安 排 合 適 保 險，當 中 涵
insurance cover for Directors’ and officers’ liabilities in respect of legal actions   蓋董事及高級人員因企業活動而產生針對彼等
against the Directors and officers arising out of corporate activities.                之法律行動之責任。

Connected Transactions                                                           關連交易
Connected transactions (also related party transactions) have been disclosed     關連交易（以及關連人士交易）已於財務報表附
in note 32 of the financial statements.                                          註32披露。


The above transactions constituted connected transactions or continuing          上述交易構成上市規則第14A章項下關連交易或
connected transactions exempted from reporting, announcement and                 持 續 關 連 交 易，獲 豁 免 遵 守 申 報、公 告 及 獨 立
independent shareholders’ approval requirements under Chapter 14A of the         股 東 批 准 規 定。董 事 認 為，上 述 交 易 乃 按 正 常
Listing Rules. The Directors are of the opinion that the above transactions      商業條款及按公平基準訂立，而上述交易之條
were entered into on normal commercial terms and on an arm’s length basis        款對本公司股東而言屬公平合理，並符合本公
and the terms of the above transactions are fair and reasonable so far as        司及本公司股東整體利益。

The Key Audit Matter                                                            關鍵審計事項
The Group’s inventories, which consist of various information technology        於 二 零 二 五 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日， 貴 集 團 的 存

Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value.           存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者的較低者列賬。


Management estimates the net realisable values of inventories based on          管理層會根據預計需求、未來售價以及實現銷
expected demand, future selling prices and the future expenses necessary        售所需的未來開支估計存貨的可變現淨值。科
to make the sales. Future demand and selling prices of technology products      技產品的未來需求及售價很大程度上會受到各
are highly susceptible to factors such as industry standards, technological     種 瞬 息 萬 變 的 因 素 影 響，例 如 業 界 標 準、科 技
changes and customer preferences, which are constantly changing and could       轉變及客戶喜好等。
change very rapidly.


Determining provision for inventories requires the exercise of significant      在 釐 定 存 貨 撥 備 時，管 理 層 需 作 出 重 大 判 斷，
management judgement as actual selling prices of individual inventory items     因為個別存貨項目的實際售價可能跌穿成本或
may fall below their costs or carrying values.                                  賬面值。


We identified the net realisable value of inventories as a key audit matter     我們將存貨可變現淨值列為關鍵審計事項，因
because any write-down or provision may have a significant impact on the        為任何撇減或撥備均可能對綜合財務報表造成
consolidated financial statements and because the estimation of the net         重大影響，且估計可變現淨值涉及管理層的重
realisable values involves the exercise of significant management judgement     大判斷，而其本質可能涉及不確定因素。
which can be inherently uncertain.

     technology (“IT”) product channel and provision of technical solution          資訊科技產品渠道及提供技術解決方案整
     integration services. The three major business segments are enterprise         和服務。三項主要業務為企業系統（提供

     and support services; Consumer electronics which provides finished             修及支援服務）；消費電子（提供資訊科技
     IT products; and cloud computing which provides cloud computing                產品整機）；及雲計算（提供雲計算解決方
     solutions and services.                                                        案和服務）。

     Islands. Its principal place of business is at Unit 3312, 33rd Floor, China    司。其 主 要 營 業 地 點 位 於 香 港 干 諾 道 中

               or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past                              行有關責任可能需要經濟利益

        Control is conveyed where the customer has both the right to                          合 約 屬 於 租 賃 或 包 含 租 賃。倘 客 戶
        direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all                同時有權指示已識別資產之用途及

3. Financial Risk Management and Fair Values                                     3. 財務風險管理及公平值
   3.1 Financial risk factors                                                         3.1 財務風險因素
       The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks                    本 集 團 之 業 務 承 受 外 匯 風 險、利 率
       including foreign exchange risk, interest rate risk, credit risk and                風 險、信 貸 風 險 及 流 動 性 風 險 等 多
       liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management program                         項 財 務 風 險。本 集 團 的 整 體 風 險 管
       focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks                      理計劃著重金融市場之不可預測性，
       to minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial                      並尋求盡量減輕對本集團財務表現
       performance.                                                                        之潛在不利影響。

             and liabilities are mainly denominated in the functional                            其資產及負債主要以集團實

             (“PHP”) and Indonesian Rupiah (“RP”).

             of reporting period is mainly arising from recognised assets                        風險主要來自以本集團實體功
             or liabilities denominated in the United States Dollar (“US$”),                     能貨幣以外之貨幣美元計值的
             a currency other than the Group’s entities’ functional                              已確認資產或負債（詳情於財
             currencies, as disclosed elsewhere in the financial                                 務報表其他章節披露）。
             statements.

             (except for HK$ which is pegged to US$) at 31 December                              能貨幣（與美元掛鈎的港元除
             2025 would have decreased/increased the post-tax profit                             外）升 值╱貶 值5 %，會 令 本

4. Accounting Estimates and Judgements                                             4. 會計估計及判斷
     (a) Estimated write-down of inventories to net                                 (a) 估 計 存 貨 撇 減 至 可 變 現
         realisable value                                                               淨值
           The Group writes down inventories to net realisable value based             本集團根據存貨可變現性之評估撇
           on an assessment of the realisability of inventories. Write-down of         減 存 貨 至 可 變 現 淨 值。倘 出 現 顯 示
           inventories is recorded to the extent where events or changes in            有關結餘可能未能變現之事件或情
           circumstances indicate that the balances may not be realised.               況 變 動，則 會 以 此 為 限 將 存 貨 撇 減
                                                                                       記賬。


           The identification of write-down requires the use of judgement              識 別 撇 減 須 運 用 判 斷 及 估 計。倘 預
           and estimates. Where the expectation is different from the                  期 有 別 於 原 有 估 計，則 有 關 差 額 將
           original estimate, such difference will impact the carrying value of        影響該估計出現變動期間之存貨賬
           inventories and write-down of inventories in the period in which            面值及存貨撇減。
           such estimate are changed.


     (b) Estimated loss allowance of trade receivables                              (b) 估 計 貿 易 應 收 款 項 虧 損
                                                                                        撥備
           The Group measures loss allowance of trade receivables at an                本集團按照金額相等於貿易應收款
           amount equal to lifetime expected credit loss of trade receivables.         項整個存續期預期信貸虧損計算貿
                                                                                       易應收款項的虧損撥備。


           The measurement of loss allowance of trade receivables requires             計量貿易應收款項的虧損撥備須運
           the use of judgement and estimates. Where the expectation is                用 判 斷 及 估 計。倘 預 期 有 別 於 原 有
           different from the original estimate, such difference will impact the       估 計，則 有 關 差 額 將 影 響 該 估 計 出
           carrying value of trade receivables and the expected credit loss in         現變動期間之貿易應收款項賬面值
           the period in which such estimates are changed.                             及預期信貸虧損。

22. Revenue and Segment Information                                            22. 收益及分部資料
   Segment information has been prepared in a manner consistent                      分部資料乃按照與本集團之最高層行政管
   with the information used by the Group’s most senior executive                    理人員就評估分部表現及於分部間分配資
   management for the purposes of assessing segment performance                      源所用資料一致之方式編製。本集團之最
   and allocating resources between segments. The Group’s most                       高層行政管理人員為董事會。就此而言，
   senior executive management has been identified as the Board. In this             本集團之高層行政管理人員按以下基準監
   regard, the Group’s senior executive management monitors the results              察各可報告分部應佔之業績：
   attributable to each reportable segment on the following bases:


   Revenue and expenses are allocated to the reportable segments with                收益及開支參照可報告分部產生之銷售額
   reference to sales generated by those segments and the expenses                   及該等分部產生之開支分配至該等分部。
   incurred by those segments.


   The measure used for reporting segment profit is segment results,                 報告分部溢利所用之計量方法為分部業
   including items specifically attributed to individual segments, such as           績，包括個別分部特別應佔之項目，例如
   directors’ and auditors’ remuneration and other administration costs              董事薪酬、核數師酬金及其他分部內行政
   within the segment.                                                               費用。


   Information regarding the Group’s reportable segments for the                     年內本集團就資源分配及分部表現評估所
   purposes of resource allocation and assessment of segment                         用之可報告分部資料載於下文。
   performance for the year is set out below.

   and aims to improve the transparency and comparability of information               準 則 第1號財 務 報 表 的 呈 列 ，並 旨 在 提 高

   classify all income and expenses into five categories in the statement of           18號，實 體 須 在 損 益 表 中 將 所 有 收 入 和

   operations and income tax categories. Entities are also required to                 資、已終止經營及所得稅。實體亦需要在
   provide specific disclosures about management-defined performance                   財務報表的單獨附註中具體披露有關管理
   measures in a single note in the financial statements.                              層定義的績效指標。
